One context helped me get a lot of subscribers. And now the question is, how to make the context interesting? There are six points that make your context interesting. The first point is a big goal. That is, to earn an apartment, a car, to take my family, to rest in Thailand or somewhere else, to move to Argentina. If there is a big goal, The context is interesting. If the goal is small, then the context is also not interesting. For example, it would be interesting for you to see how a millionaire of dollars says, I will earn for an apartment. Of course not, because he is already a millionaire. But if, for example, a homeless guy or, for example, some student who has no money with him, talked about how I will earn an apartment in the center of Moscow in six months. This will be interesting, because the goal is very big for him. The second point is a limitation. I will earn an apartment sounds normal. Cool. But if I add a limitation here, in the form of earning an apartment for 3 months with $ 1000 at the start. That is, I added even two restrictions. Deadline and budget restrictions. And as a result, the goal seems unfulfilled, which makes the context more interesting. The third point is dissonance. Let's say I lose weight by 10 kg. It sounds normal. Losing weight by 10 kg per month sounds more interesting, because there is a deadline. But if I say that I lose weight by 10 kg per month only on fast food, adding dissonance here, it's even more interesting. How can it be that you eat fast food and lose weight by 10 kg per month? It's unreal, it's impossible. What the ****? But this is what makes the context interesting. 4. Character or personality. Let's say I say that I want to buy my father a car. Sounds cool, but if I add the story of my father here, seeing that my father refused himself all his life to provide for me, so that I would be dressed, so that I would go to school normally, so that there would be something else. And in the end, I want to repay him with the fact that I gave him a dream car. Then there is already a story of the character, there is a story of a person. The motive and purpose are much clearer. The context is also interesting. The fifth point is the precedent of the fact that this has never happened before. For example, we earn plus 2 million rubles from 19 reels live. For example, before us, no one made such money from such a number of reels in a month. It was Unreal, this has never happened before, and in the end, thanks to this context, I gained a lot of coverage. Even the video with reality got 800 ,000 views each, or 900 ,000 views, although there was no benefit. And we made a great launch. And he did so. He gained 50 kilograms, filmed about it in real estate, and on these 50 kilograms he lost weight, and when he started selling a course on weight loss, he made billions of sales. In this lesson, we will understand how to write scenarios. First, I will show you what a basic scenario scheme looks like, then I will explain the logic of writing scenarios, and then we will go through the types of content, that is, the expert type of content, I will show the template, the personal type, the template, the expert-seller and the seller type of content. There will be a lot of templates, there will be a lot of examples, so the lesson will be a little long and a little difficult. But after this lesson, you will have no reason not to write great scenarios. So let's get started right away. The most basic scenario scheme looks like this. The first point is the hook, that is, to catch attention in 3-5 seconds. Everyone knows this, I will not stop here, I have already said this many times. The second point is the update, that is, to give a reason to watch the video to the end. Why is it important? A person leaves thousands of reels a day, and it is very important to make your reels catch him with a hook and then give him motivation to watch to the end. That is, I must understand that in the end I will get something, I will receive a reward in the form of emotions, in the form of inspiration, in the form of some insight, in the form of benefit. If I immediately at the beginning, at the moment of the update, I will know that this video will be paid off, That is, I will work hard for this time, but in the end I will get my reward. Then I will review it. That is, we must give this motivation. If I immediately realize that the video is some strange, muddy, useless, I will not review it. Therefore, actualization is very important. That is, after the hook, you can cause intrigue, hook pain, make it clear that there will be benefit, and so on. After the actualization, we have the main part, that is, we reveal the main meaning. There are steps, there are problems, there are solutions. Everything is the most juicy, the most interesting should be right here. But at the end of the conclusion, that is, we summarize. At the moment of conclusion, there may be your opinion, there may be some final insight, or even a result. For example, I show the whole video of how I cook food, and in the conclusion I show what came out in the end, and I show the reactions of my relatives to this burger. And the last point is call to action. That is, we translate a person into a subscription, a funnel, applications, and so on. With the basic scheme, everything is clear. --- УРОК 9 ---
In this lesson, we will analyze the topic of shooting, namely, the angles of the composition, so that your videos are more dynamic, more beautiful, more unique. We will analyze what is A-roll, B-roll and C-roll. You will understand this later. We will analyze what is decoding and why it is very important. We will analyze the mistakes you make when shooting. I will also tell you what kind of equipment you need. And I will tell you how I shot a video for 1 million views on the phone at home in just 30 minutes. So, let's start with angles and composition. Before, when I was just learning to shoot, always angles and composition were shown in a horizontal format, because then few people shot reels. In general, I could not find anywhere vertical examples of angles and composition, so I collected everything myself, manually from other reels, so that it was clearer and more convenient for you to repeat it in the future. Let's start with the angle, then move on to the composition. First, I will tell you what is the difference between angles and composition. Angle is the angle at which the camera shoots you. That is, the angle is about the camera, and the composition is what is inside the frame. That is, how you stand, what you have on the background, and so on. Now, for example, you will understand everything. So, the first most common angle in real life and cinema in general is the angle at eye level, when the camera is at the same level with your eyes. Here are a few examples where the camera is just at the level of my eyes. This angle helps to create the feeling that you are very close to the viewer. You are not stronger than him, you are not weaker than him, you are at the same level with him. You get a visual contact. Here are a couple of examples where in real life I used just the same. Plus you have a lot of space above the head. Here are some more examples. And everywhere you have a camera at the level of the eye. Good angle, but too often it is used, so this angle you do not directly stand out among other videos in the recommendation list. So let's move on to the angles that are a little more interesting. Angle number two. The shot from above. When the camera shoots you from above, directed down. In general, this is one of my favorite shots, because so few people shot when I started. Because, first of all, it's a little difficult. You need a high tripod, which is also bent down. Therefore, few people want to steam and so they shoot very rarely. Therefore... I'll add some more uniqueness later. Here's an example where I needed to show a lot of my copies. If I shot it at eye level, then I wouldn't have time to insert these copies. --- УРОК 12 ---
Hello everyone, this is a lesson on sales funnels and in this lesson we will analyze how sales funnels work through Reels, what elements they consist of and which funnel is best to choose for your product and for the audience that you have in your blog. What is the purpose of this lesson? The goal of this lesson is to figure out how to get from 100 potential clients per month on your products and services with the help of sales funnels, and also to understand how to make $ 10 ,000 through funnels the easiest way. We will go from simple to complex. That is, first we will analyze the basis of what are funnels and from which elements. They consist of... And then we will analyze concrete examples of craters. So, what is a crater? A crater is a path from the moment when a person saw us for the first time in life to the purchase. Let's analyze an example of a crater. For example, a person wants to buy jeans. And we are a store that sells jeans. And what does a person need to walk through a crater and eventually buy jeans from us? He needs to take five steps. The first step is to come to the shopping mall where we are. The second step is to choose a clothing store and enter it. Choose our clothing store. The third step is to choose a model of jeans in size and price that he liked. The fourth step is to try on the jeans. And the fifth step is to buy these jeans if the jeans fit the person and everyone liked it. And in the end, all these steps from the moment when a person just came to the shopping center to the moment when he tried on jeans and bought them, they are just called a funnel. What is a funnel made of? A funnel consists of three elements. The first element is traffic, the second is a converter, and the third is sales. Traffic is where the potential client learns about our existence, in principle, and sees us for the first time. How can he see us for the first time? He can see us through advertising on Target or through advertising on Telegram. either through advertising in Google or Yandex, or someone can recommend us to him, his friends or acquaintances. Or he can come to the shopping center and see our store there, if we have some offline product. Or he can go to YouTube and see our video on YouTube. Or he can find us through Reels or through Shorts and also see us in the recommendations. And look, Reels themselves, in fact, will never bring you money on their own. Why? Because Reels is just one of the elements in the funnel. That is, Reels in any funnel close one of the most important blocks, which is called traffic. And it for free gives people just the opportunity to see us. And that's it. But Reels themselves sell badly. Reels themselves do not bring practically never money. They just give people the opportunity to find out about you and go into your funnel to then warm up and buy your product. And just the second stage, where a person warms up and makes a decision to leave an application or buy a product, is a converter. A converter is an element where a person warms up, learns about our product, understands whether he wants to buy it or not, and from a cold potential client, a person turns into an application. What are the conversion options? It can be some kind of sales presentation, pdf. Or a person can come to our showroom and see the products that we have. Or a person can go to our website and find out about our product. Or come to the webinar and also find out about our course. Or he can be subscribed to us on Instagram and he can watch our stories. And after the stories, also make a decision about buying or about leaving an application. Or it can be a video on YouTube. Or it can be a Telegram channel where you also post some kind of warming content. All these are examples of different types of converters. And the last stage of the funnel is sales. After a person leaves an application, he already buys. How can he buy? He can buy through auto payment. Auto payment is when a person goes to the bot himself, or to the site, gets acquainted with the product himself, puts in the requisites of his card and pays himself. Here we do not need to contact him through the sales department, we do not need to sell personally and so on. A person can just see your club by subscription, go to the Telegram bot, click the payment button, enter the requisites and pay. This is called auto payment. The second option is through the sales department, when a person leaves an application, our managers contact him and sell it to him. The third option is personal sales. This is when we take it, contact this person ourselves, close it on a call and sell it on the call. And the fourth option is through direct, when a person closes for payment through correspondence. And look, what is important, where do we need to focus when we make any funnel. Our focus should not be on making sales, because the number of sales is difficult to control and difficult to count. We do not understand how to influence the number of sales. Our focus should be to increase the conversion of each step of the funnel and make the maximum number of people enter the funnel. Let's look at the example with the purchase of jeans. For example, we have two stores. The first store is H &M, the second store is Zara. And let's see how some micro deviations in percentage affect the total amount that each store earns. The first store is H &M. Here, 10 ,000 people came to the shopping center, 5 % of people went to the H &M store. This is 500 people out of 10 ,000. They chose some jeans for 20 % of people, that's 100 people. They chose jeans and went to try them on. As a result, they tried on these jeans for 90 % of people, that's 90 people. And 50 % of people who tried on jeans, as a result, bought them. That's 45 people. And let's take an average check for jeans for 5000 rubles. It turns out that the H &M store earned 225 ,000 rubles from 500 people who went to this store. And let's take an example with Zara. Here, at each stage, the difference between Zara and the H &M store in conversion is only a few percent. That is, instead of 5 % of people who went to the H &M store, only 6 % of people went to Zara, only 1 % more. The number of people who chose jeans is only 2 % more. The number of people who tried on jeans is 5 % more. The number of people who bought jeans is also 5 % more. But in the end, the revenue that Zara received is already 120 ,000 rubles. This is about 50 % of H &M. In this case, Zara would earn 50 % more than H &M, although the conversion rate at each stage from the H &M store are not much different. That's why it's important to understand what numbers you have in the funnel, it's important to understand where there are some narrow corners, after which people leave your funnel and lose interest in the product, and to be able to make sure that at each stage the maximum number of people passes to the next stage. Let's now figure out how to choose a funnel in two steps. To choose a funnel, you first need to determine the key stage of the funnel. The key stage of the funnel is some event after which a person becomes a client. If you don't know what your kef is, you can either complicate the funnel too much, for example, do a webinar to sell a marathon for losing weight for 1500 rubles. And it will not work well. Why? Because it's too difficult for such a low check. That is, a person will hardly spend 2-2.5 hours of the webinar to make a purchase for 1500 rubles. And potentially a person, to make a decision to buy a product for 1500 rubles, could be enough for only one real estate. or one short video on YouTube for 10 minutes. Why? Because this purchase decision is not stressful, there is a small check and a person does not need a lot of arguments to buy a product for 1500 rubles. Also, if you do not know your kef, you can, on the contrary, greatly simplify the funnel. For example, you can sell some advice for 500 ,000 rubles just from the site. And if you have a simple button on the site to buy, and there you will need to enter the requisites, after which a person is charged 500 ,000 rubles, it will be strange. And there will be a very small conversion in payment, even if you have a really cool product. Simply because to buy some product for 500 ,000 rubles, a person most often needs to at least interact with another living person. to communicate with you, or with the manager, or with someone else, just to understand that, yes, there really is such a service or such a product, and yes, these are really living people, whom you can trust, and pay them 500 ,000. And if we just enter the details on the site, it will not work. That is why the first step is to understand our CAF, in order to choose the right funnel for CAF. Let's look at some examples. We will not only look at examples in the field of online and infobusiness, but also in any other fields. For example, the kitchen for an order. Here, the key stage of the funnel is a calculation of the cost of the project. And after that, the person makes a decision to order a kitchen. In dentistry, this is a free diagnosis. A person comes to the clinic, makes x-rays of his jaw and generally makes a calculation, says what problems with the teeth there are, what needs to be treated. In terms of infoproducts, it can be an application for a pre-recorded questionnaire, and if it's clothes, then it's a visit to the showroom with a fitting, maybe. If it's flowers, then it's a direct message or WhatsApp. That is, if a person wrote some kind of direct message where he was interested in a specific bouquet, then after that, most often through correspondence, he closes in on the client. Sales of machines is a test drive. If it is a sales of services, then it is a salesman with a qualified application. What is a qualified application? When we understand that a person really has a request for a product, for a service, when we understand that he has some budgets and he is targeted for us. And these are examples of cavs, after which a person becomes a client in business. Now let's figure out how to understand which CAF should we have. CAF is an event that, first of all, takes place one step before the purchase. That is, if you are already selling some of your products, then you need to understand after what event most often people become customers. And second, this event brings the most money. When we define a CAF, our task is to make sure that the maximum number of people reach it. And the CAF should be only one, just so that we have a focus on some one key action that we need to multiply, and we have to add only this. For example, we understand that we sell through webinars. And the main thing we need to do is just make sure that the maximum number of people reach the webinar. And we understand that if, for example, 1 ,000 people reach the webinars, then from 1 ,000 people we will earn $50 ,000. For example, if the webinar reaches 10 ,000 people, then from 10 ,000 people, respectively, we will earn $ 500 ,000, for example. And look, there should be only one calf, but the crows that lead to this calf can be a lot. That is, if the calf is some kind of lake from which we catch fish, then the sources that fall into this lake, rivers, there can be a large number of them, a large number of traffic sources, applications, and so on. For example, pre-recorded applications can be obtained from stories, from the Telegram channel, from some videos on YouTube, and so on. That is, there are a lot of options for how we bring people to our key stage of the funnel, but the key stage of the funnel should always remain the same. And online there are three of the most working key stages of the funnel that can be used. The first option is an application or a pre -recorded questionnaire. It is best suited for some mass products with a check of $500 with a plus. Here, sales take place through the sales department. That is, a person leaves an application, managers contact him, make a presentation and sell the product. The second option is a personal sale or diagnosis. It is best suited for the sale of services, for the sale of some premium products or when there is a small audience. Here we already sell personally by phone or through correspondence. The third option is any kev through auto payment. For example, through a selling article, or through a website, or through a webinar. Let me remind you what auto payment is. Auto payment is when a person has looked at some of our marketing materials and went to our website or our bot, made a request and paid for the product himself. Best of all, it all works for some inexpensive products. Now let's figure out how to make a million dollars in revenue thanks to understanding the key stage of the funnel. In general, a million dollars in revenue sounds like some kind of super big goal. That is, it seems that this is really a lot. But in fact, if you understand CAF and if you understand the figures of your funnel, then a million dollars turn into some fairly simple task at the calculation level. Let's take data from our project with Altair. It turns out that 6 % of people who left an application, that is, reached the key stage of the funnel, eventually buy the product. That is, out of 100 people who left an application, 6 % become customers. These are the data from one of our last launches, where we made a little less than $300 ,000. At this launch, we made 245 sales out of about 4000 applications. And now let's count how to make a million dollars starting from these numbers. Here we need a reverse decomposition. This is when we count the actions that we need to do at each stage from the goal. Our goal is, for example, $1 million. The average check on our product is about $1 ,150. To make $1 ,000 ,000 in revenue, we need to make 870 sales. Since we have a 6 % conversion from the application to payment, we need to make 14 ,500 applications. If we want to make $1 ,000 ,000 in revenue per year, we divide 14 ,500 applications by 365 days, and that's about 40 applications. And here we have a magic. Our goal, which is complicated and incomprehensible in the million dollars that we need to make, turned into a fairly simple goal that can be influenced, which is clear how to do. These are 40 applications every day. We can influence these 40 applications and there are clear tools how to do it. For example, in our project, the record for the number of applications per day was 646 applications. That is, we completed the standard of 13 days That is, in one day we made as many applications as we should do, in theory, for 13-14 days in order to make $ 1 million a year. And now every action we make on our project, it comes down to this simple number of 40 applications a day. And now we evaluate each of our actions only by whether it will increase the number of people at the key stage of the funnel or not. For example, we decided to shoot some Reels, and we think, will the dance for trendy music bring me closer to 40 applications per day or not? And here it is obvious that the dance for music will hardly bring you closer to 40 applications per day. And what will bring you closer? The funnel will bring you closer. And let's move on. The second step is to choose a funnel for the key stage of the funnel. The key stage of the funnel should be one. We determined it in the previous step, but there may be several funnels that lead to it. For example, in order to make 4000 applications for a project with Altair, we used about 8-9 different tools to get these applications. But in your case, now at the stage at which you are, it will be enough to make one good funnel. And start with this, and then add some more tools. And now we will analyze which funnels should be done in priority, and which funnels are better to postpone and not to do at all. Which funnels do not work? The first is if you make a funnel for funnels. That is, you think that a bot in Instagram or a bot in Telegram is already a funnel. But in fact, this is not so, because a funnel is what brings applications and brings people to purchase. The second thing that doesn't work is any subscription bonuses. For example, you give some kind of font pack for a subscription, or some kind of guide, or anything else you give for a subscription, it doesn't work. Because the goal of Voronka, once again, is to warm a person up to the product and get an application or payment, and not to get a subscriber who needs a font pack or sounds. That is, I always, when I transfer people to Voronka, I immediately understand that this Voronka Does it bring a person directly to the application or not? If it does not bring, then it is inefficient. There is a point in making some bonuses for the subscription, not as separate funnels, but when you, for example, just live your life, you decided to make some kind of Reels, for example, on the topic of finances, and you already use some kind of table for finances. And since you already have a table for finances, you just give this table for finances as a bonus to people who watched your reels. Then yes, it can be used, just because you spent little effort and time on it. And if you need to make some kind of 50-page guide as a lead magnet, then it makes little sense, because a 50-page guide in the format of a lead magnet is ineffective. it rarely converts people to applications, it rarely warms people. It is much better to make one video. Therefore, we always give priority to those types of funnels that do not lead to subscribers, but lead to applications and potential clients. In my projects, we, perhaps, in 5-10 % of cases, sometimes use some bonuses for subscription, and everything else we always lead to other funnels, which we will also analyze. The next thing that should not be done and that does not work is a tripwire funnel. Tripwire is some kind of cheap product that has a high value for a person. For example, it can be some kind of marathon, some kind of event, and so on. Why can't you sell tripwires, most likely, at these stages? Because tripwires, they are poorly sold when you have small grips. And if you have not yet made at least a million rubles clean, then it is better not to sell tripwires at this stage. Why? Because the first million rubles from the blog is much easier to make not on a cheap product, but on an expensive product. It is much easier to make 10 sales for $ 1 ,000 than to make 333 sales for $ 30. Just because to make 333 sales for $ 30, you need to go crazy what a huge amount of traffic. And if you have such a potential audience of your customers, if you sell them an expensive product, you will most likely earn even more. Therefore, in your place, until you have made at least a million rubles clean, I would definitely not deal with any triple-A holes, but first I would make a flagship, good, expensive product. set up sales in it, and after that, I was already thinking about what kind of tripwire funnels there could be. The next thing you shouldn't do is funnels through marathons or auto-webinars. And why? Because these are too long and difficult funnels. And at the start, when you just start, it's also better not to do them. Why? Simply because instead of one webinar for 3 hours, you can make 5 videos on YouTube for 30 minutes. And look, if you do a webinar for 3 hours, then only your subscribers will be able to get to this webinar, who will follow the link, go to this webinar and watch it. And it may turn out that you did one webinar for 3 hours for 50 or 100 people. If you make 5 videos on YouTube for 30 minutes, then these 5 videos for 30 minutes can potentially see several tens of thousands of people. Because these videos will additionally see not only your subscribers, but also people on Organic. And from this you will get much more applications. And after you have some already accumulated base, some large number of subscribers, just to create another additional funnel, you can, in principle, hold some webinars, marathons, and so on. But only when you already have some adequate audience for which you will do it. Adequate in terms of the number of this audience. Now let's talk about what works and what doesn't. We've talked about what doesn't work. Making funnels for funnels, making funnels with bonuses for subscription, making tripwire funnels, making marathons or auto-webinars. Approximately according to the figures, that what it gives the same results in money. About sales. How do we sell? KEF is the applications that go to the sales department. Simply because people all this time in any marketing materials and in any funnel have seen and heard an expert. But if you already have more than 100 applications per month, then you stop selling personally and hire at least one manager and sell using the sales department. Just to save your time. What numbers can you focus on here? Here I took and made a comparative table with two variants of funnel. The first variant of the funnel is when we sell personally. This is the last funnel that we had. And the second variant of the funnel is when we sell through the sales department. Just because it is a less effective funnel at individual conversion stages. That is, a smaller number of people will leave applications and a smaller conversion in payment will also be. And according to the table, it can be seen that personal sales are several times more effective. But why do we sell on our projects, for example, through the sales department? Because in one and a half months we received 4 ,000 applications on our project with Altair. And let's count what would happen if we tried to sell all these 4 ,000 people personally. If we were doing sales for these 4 ,000 people personally, then 4 ,000 people that need to be processed are 4 ,000 working hours. Because on average, with personal sales, one person spends about an hour. Sometimes less, sometimes more. And it turns out, if Altaïr himself is an expert, would sell the product to all these 4 ,000 people, he would need 500 working days in a row with an 8-hour working schedule. That is, he would have to work for a year and a half in a row with these 4 ,000 applications every day, do only this, do not deal with the product at all, do not release new content, just sit and sell. And do only this for 1.5 years, every day for 8 hours. Can you imagine? That is, it is physically impossible to do this with a large number of applications. And that is why it is important to switch to the sales department over time and refuse personal sales in principle. Because yes, personal sales can bring you more money at the moment, but if you do not refuse this, you will not be able to do more important things, such as strategies, creating content, creating a product, and so on. And think about it, if Steve Jobs was in an Apple store behind the counter, then the store would bring 10 times more money, most likely, if Steve Jobs himself worked as a sales consultant. But think about it, if Steve Jobs himself was in sales, then Steve Jobs would never have become Steve Jobs, whom we know, he would not have created the iPhone, simply because he would not be the head of the company, Not an inventor, not a visionary, he would be just a salesman. And so you always need to give up the routine story with sales, when you do it yourself, hand it over to the sales department after some volume and, yes, lose money, but grow in the number of applications, in scale, and so on. And the number of people who are on your product. The next and last version of the funnel is the funnel when we work with our base of subscribers who came to us and immediately transfer this base to the key stage of the funnel or to the application. That is, people who have passed our YouTube funnel, for example, watched our videos, left an application, and so on. Of the total revenue that we make per year, 30-40 % of people will only buy our product after the first touch. And the other 60-70 % of sales we will make from the base during the year. And it all happens with the base after people have already come to your funnel, have already left an application, someone has already bought, someone has not bought. You also continue to warm up this base for many more months and years. When do you need to start working with the base in general? It is better to start working with the base when you already have thousands of subscribers. Before that time, I would absolutely direct the whole focus on doing only videos on YouTube and doing only Reels, and almost not to have stories, almost not to have a Telegram channel, and so on. Why? Because it just makes no sense. There is no point in doing some weekly warm-ups in stories and write down what cool product you have if 100 people are watching you. And the next time you go out in stories, you will tell some selling meanings not for 100 people already, but already for 200 or 300 people, and it will be much more effective. What conversions are there to work with the base on average? Here I will take our conversions on the project. On average, we get from 1 % to 10 % conversions per application from covers, from each warm-up. That is, we took, wrote some kind of selling post, or made some kind of selling storytel in stories, and from this we get 1-10 % conversions per application. For example, if we have covers in stories, let's say 10 ,000 people watched to the last story, then it turns out that from 10 ,000 people who watched our warm-up and reached the last story with the application, we have on average from 100 to 1000 people leave this application. These are the same numbers with posts. That is, if a post in Telegram scored 5000 covers, for example, then it turns out that from 50 to 500 applications can potentially be received from a post for 5000 covers. And so from each post that you publish, the main thing is that these posts are with different selling meanings, which affect a different target audience, different pains, and so on. Let's now summarize which funnel is best to choose. The funnel from realsow immediately leads to the key stage of the funnel, when it needs to be chosen. In the first case, if you have a mass and inexpensive product, it makes sense. Also, if you sell it for some free of charge. This works when you have an expensive product and more than 100 applications per month. A funnel when we work with a base and transfer the base to the key stage of the funnel or to the application. This is worth doing when you already have more than 1000 subscribers. When you have few subscribers, there is no point in working with a base, it is better to make content, reels and YouTube. It's like watching a series from the second season. Well, like, from the second season it's hard to watch a series because you don't understand anything. And you have to watch from the first season, where you are explained who the character is, why all this mess was set up, and what is the motivation for whom. And in your storyline should be the same, you should always explain first. Plus, along the course of the storyline, periodically remind what it all started with and what details are in this situation. Second, people must understand your motivation and why it is important for you. For example, I did not just decide to get rid of Kazimir, but I show the situation that, firstly, he was given to us when we did not ask for it, and secondly, he **** everything, ****, and when he slipped on this ladder, he runs, screams without stopping. That is, I show my motivation, people can sympathize with me, understand what I feel, And this story looks easier. And so in any line, not just I buy an apartment, but why it is important to me to buy an apartment, why I will feel safe there, or this is my dream from childhood, or something else. In general, you always need to explain why it is important to you. And thirdly, people should understand why you make those or other decisions, you always need to explain your choice. You know, they often say that it is necessary to broadcast values ​​in the blog. And how to broadcast them? No one says. In fact, it is very simple. People judge you and your values ​​at the moments when you make some choice and explain it. When you say, I was offered to advertise a bookmaker's office for this amount, I can earn a million, but I can refuse and not do it. I choose not to do this, because I respect you, I value you and I don't want you to lose money. When you show the choice and show what choice you made and explain why, at this moment your value is manifested. Therefore, three important rules of plot lines are that people must understand the situation and the details, you need to make a prehistory and always immerse people in the context. Secondly, you always need to explain why it is important for you to explain your motivation. And thirdly, when there is some kind of choice, you must say that here is the choice, I need to do this or that, I will do this because and explain. And really the best lifehack on stories for my whole life It is to designate 5 long storylines and regularly update them. In this case, you will always have something to talk about. For example, a line about how you get into a relationship, a line about how you throw a ****, a line about how you try to earn the first million, a short and funny line about what you want to learn to stand on your hands and try to do it, and another line with the fact that the neighbors constantly have a drill. And that's it, you just updated this line in one day, in the other, in the third, showed the drill, and so round and round and round. And it will be really interesting to watch behind your blog. By the way, did you think about what distinguishes an interesting blogger whose stories you watch from an uninteresting blogger whose stories you do not watch? In fact, they are also different, which distinguishes the first series of the series from the last. Why? The last series of the series is much more interesting to watch than The difference is how many details you know. When you know a lot of details about a person, it's interesting to look at him. For example, when Morgenstern is parting with someone, this is news that is interesting to discuss with everyone, because we know a lot of details about Morgenstern. And when some unknown person leaves, we are not interested, because we do not know anything about him, and this is generally something incomprehensible. And a very common problem of low coverage is that people do not understand the context of your life. You just go out and show there, I'm sitting, working, but they do not understand who you are, what you are working on, where did you start, where are you going. And for them it's just a picture where a man is sitting at the computer, and it's not interesting to watch. And when they know your backstory, know your storyline, know what you're doing now, it's a series, and then it's not just a picture of you sitting at the computer, it's part of the series, and then it's interesting to watch. Well, let's move on to the next topic, now we will understand how to do warm-ups. People often complain to me, Misha, I post useful content, but for some reason they don't buy it. I answer, yes, because people usually don't buy from useful content, they buy from warming and selling content. what is the difference between them useful content it is just useful for example three exercises on the biceps and useful content creates trust a person begins to understand that you understand this topic but this is not enough to want to buy a warming content is content for example how my client lost weight by 20 kilograms and warming content creates a desire to buy and to have big sales we need to learn to do not only useful content, but also warming up content. Okay, what is warming up? Warming up is to convey to subscribers a list of the necessary meanings, after which people themselves will want to buy. I imagine it like this, that we put check marks in a person's head. He sits, looks at you and thinks, okay, but the sphere that you are engaged in, is it cool at all? I will like it. And we tell him, yes, it's cool. once put a check mark, he is like, will I really be able to, and we say, yes, you will definitely be able to, here are examples of students for you, he is like, and if I am completely a beginner, we are like, yes, beginners also get it, here is an example, and we put a check mark, he is like, okay, and does it bring money, yes, they brought the meaning, okay, did I get into a cool community, yes, they brought the meaning, okay, and this is a growing niche, yes, they brought the meaning, and when we bring the right amount of meaning, we put all these check marks in in a person's head, he starts to treat what we offer him differently and wants to buy it. In general, warm-up is content that leads the audience from the state of who you are to the state of wow, cool, I want to buy it. And warming-up content is published everywhere. And in the stories, for example, at launch, most often I do warm-up in the stories, active for 10-30 days. And on YouTube, for example, you can publish 2-4 large warming-up videos on your topic. And in reels you can make 5-10 warming up reels that will sell what you sell. And you can in Telegram, for example, duplicate the meaning from reels and stories from YouTube, in posts in Telegram and in Telegram also do warming up. I am for the fact that the warm-up came out on all your sites that you have. In general, we can talk about warming up for a very long time and a lot. I have been actively engaged in this since 2019 and have probably spent more than 10 ,000 hours on it. But if we generalize the whole essence of warming up, that is, what is its basis and logic, then warming up is only six key steps. First, we need to show a personal example of the result that you have achieved what a person wants to achieve. For example, here is what kind of press I have pumped, or here is how I have grown my blog, or here is how much money I have earned. Or here are the great teeth I made for the client. Secondly, you need to explain to people how they can come to this result. For example, yes, I got 10 ,000 subscribers and there is a clear step-by-step plan how to repeat it. Step one is like this, step two is like this, third, fourth, fifth. This is my method, we do it like this. Do the same and you will also come to this. To sell what you sell, people must understand very well how to get there. And so when you explain to people step by step how to get to the results that they want to come, people buy you much better. The third element of the warm-up, we need to confirm everything you said with examples and reviews of other people. to tell that my results and my method work not only on me, but also on other people. That one person made all these steps that I'm talking about, and he got the same result. Here's the second person, here's the third, and here's their feedback, what they say about it. And they say, wow, cool, I didn't even think it would be so easy and so awesome. The fourth element of warm-up is to gain confidence by explaining individual pieces of the way in detail. For example, my method is that you need to do 6 steps for 10 ,000 subscribers. And I can post a video on YouTube where I will explain in detail how to do one of the steps. For example, how cool to pack your profile on Instagram. From the fact that I will explain to people how to pack their profile, they, of course, will not break their blog to 10 ,000 subscribers. In any case, they will have to buy something from me if they want to get the full scheme. But on the example of a specific detailed analysis of one of the steps, they will see that I understand it, they will be able to assume that it means that I tell other steps in the same way in detail and well, that I really understand what I am talking about, and then they will be able to buy from me. And if you look at my YouTube, then in it, in fact, I reveal in detail Separate pieces of the way. How exactly to do reels, how exactly to do stories, what to start with, what to pay attention to at the start, how to develop discipline, how to plan time. This is useful content and it is really needed by people, it will really help them on their way, but this is not the whole way. And if a person wants to go all the way, all the steps to the result and get the result, then the person is already going to my main paid product. The fifth element of warm-up is to cover fears and objections of people. What if my metabolism is slow? What if I can't draw? What if I don't have a designer taste? What if I'm from a small town? What if I have never had experience in blogging? What if I have a narrow niche? What if I can't edit? In general, in each niche there are 20 most frequent objections to what people face when they start to develop in these niches. And you need in your content show that you know these objections and you need to work out these objections in your content. They are worked out by explaining why this objection and fear is actually not true, not real and should not be afraid. And confirm it with people's examples. For example, a person may be afraid that you can't develop a blog from scratch. When the hero faces challenges, turns out to be in new situations, it's interesting. And the second thing that a hero should do in these new situations, he should act. If he just turns out to be in new situations, but does nothing, it's not interesting to watch this either. If I just say, I have a daughter, and I don't show anything else, I don't tell how life looks, I don't show what I'm doing in connection with this, then it's not interesting to watch this. And the final conclusion. If there are no ideas for content, change your life first. The next advice is similar to the previous one, but more directed inward. This advice is to work on yourself, change and inspire. Globally, there are two reasons why people can watch you. The first reason is that you make very cool content. The second reason is that you are cool yourself. And there are people on whom we are subscribed and watch them very carefully and forgive them not perfect storytelling, not perfect stories and not perfect reels just because they are our role models. Well, let's say Cristiano Ronaldo does not lead the blog perfectly, but he is the most popular account on Instagram, because tens of millions of guys around the world dream of becoming Cristiano Ronaldo. This is a hyperbolic example, but it works the same way in real life. If you are, for example, a 30-year-old man, and at the same time you look cool, you have a cool car, you live cool, you talk cool, you read a lot of books, you worked out in psychotherapy, you feel cool, you have cool relationships, a beautiful girl or a wife, maybe children, you are engaged in an interesting project, then a huge number of men will watch you just because they will want to know how to become the same. And your content can be two times worse, but watching you will still be two times more. And the reverse situation. If you can't imagine anything about yourself and just retell other people's thoughts, and even if you do it very cool and with hooks at the beginning and in different frames and apply all the rules from the content formula, but at the same time it's all not yours and you have it written on your face that you haven't done anything in your life yet, not that you haven't done it, you haven't even started doing it, people feel it. And some of your reels can be popular, even people can subscribe to you, but globally you won't have a base of followers, a base of people who want to be like you. And so the best way to keep a subscriber for a long time is to become his role model, the person he wants to be like. And this is what I've been thinking a lot about for the last 5-7 years. What should Misha Timochka be like? What should he know? What should he read? How should he look like? Who should he talk to? What should he do? So that hundreds of thousands of people want to be like Misha Timochka. Because to be similar means to be subscribed, to watch carefully and to actively buy. And it is also very important to think about this, so that your blog grows. No one is interested in just watching a talk show. for a person who makes cool scenarios with a GPT chat and then speaks them out. The fifth advice is to let people and algorithms get used to you. People come to me periodically and say, Misha, I shot 5 reels, but so far no one has subscribed to me, look, is everything okay with these reels or not? And to such people I always answer that it is too early to draw conclusions on 5 reels. This is how a person went to 5 workouts and tries to understand whether he pumped up or not. Well, for 5 workouts, you will not feel the effect. You have to walk for half a year, then look before and after, and you see, oh, it seems to be pumped up. And it's the same with reals. 3, 4, 5, 7 reals. You won't feel the effect. You need to shoot for a long time. You need to choose 1-2 formats, shoot in them for half a year, shoot a little better each time. And then, after half a year, you understand, oh, 12 ,000 new subscribers added to my blog. That's the only way it works. Because the algorithm needs to get used to you. And people need to get used to you. When you only have an empty account, the algorithms don't even know who to recommend you to. They don't have the experience that they recommended you to someone and it worked. They don't have enough statistics to offer you enough data. Let them collect this data. Just make content so that the algorithms understand who you are, what category you belong to. How to connect your content? For example, a recommendation tape. How does it work? If a person watched a similar Reels from a similar topic, then he will also be recommended by your Reels. But for this, the algorithm must understand what topic you are in and which bloggers you look like. And for this, it also takes time for the algorithms to load and understand who you are, what your content is about, to whom you can be attached, to which bloggers your content can be recommended. And for this, it may take a month, or maybe two, or maybe three. And you can shoot the same content for three months, and only from the third month your content can start to grow simply because the algorithms have finally received a sufficient amount of data and understood whom to recommend you. And secondly, people need to get used to you. Because you blinked a person once, he glanced, blinked a second time, he already looked at the beginning of the reel, blinked a third time, he looked at the reel as a whole. You blink again, the person thinks, so what kind of guy is in my tape, comes to your profile, studies reels and thinks, oh, cool, he has a lot of the same good content, so I will subscribe to him. And it really works so that you need to show yourself many times to even one person so that he subscribes to you. If your goal is to have 10 ,000 subscribers, you just need to do a lot of content, do it a lot, regularly, and after some time people will start to get used to you and subscribe to you.
